## E101 — 배보다 먼저 도착한 빚

- hull docking sequence pauses because title-holder, Helix lien and insurer send incompatible acceptance instructions; the tug cannot release physical custody without one valid receiving chain.

**REENTRY_ANCHOR**

- the outer airlock accepts three keys and still refuses to open.

- Ovel becomes named irreversible injury/aftermath obligation, not a heroic standard mascot; no usable accident-derived upgrade.
